LINE-HAUL COST COLLECTOR — FULL APP CODE
Paste order: 1 App.Formulas, 2 scrFuel, 3 scrDetails, 4 scrRecent, 5 scrEntry (use studio/scrEntry.yaml). Setup steps: README.md

==================================================================
== App.Formulas.fx
==================================================================
// Line-Haul Cost Collector · App.Formulas
// Paste into App → Formulas (append below anything already there).

// ---------- Theme: LTL Rate Request layout, teal palette and a light nav rail so the two apps aren't confused ----------
clrInk = RGBA(22, 32, 43, 1);
clrInk2 = RGBA(72, 88, 106, 1);
clrInk3 = RGBA(125, 139, 153, 1);
clrPage = RGBA(236, 243, 243, 1);
clrPanel = RGBA(255, 255, 255, 1);
clrLine = RGBA(212, 225, 226, 1);
clrSoft = RGBA(242, 248, 248, 1);
clrBlue = RGBA(14, 110, 112, 1);        // primary accent (teal)
clrBlueHover = RGBA(26, 138, 140, 1);
clrRed = RGBA(196, 22, 28, 1);
clrRedBg = RGBA(253, 236, 237, 1);
clrAmber = RGBA(242, 183, 5, 1);
clrAmberBg = RGBA(255, 247, 221, 1);
clrAmberInk = RGBA(125, 94, 0, 1);
clrGreenBg = RGBA(231, 244, 236, 1);
clrGreenInk = RGBA(20, 96, 63, 1);
clrRail = RGBA(255, 255, 255, 1);       // nav rail: light, the opposite of LTL's dark rail
clrRailOn = RGBA(14, 110, 112, 1);      // selected nav item: solid teal pill
clrRailInk = RGBA(74, 96, 98, 1);       // nav icons and labels
clrRailOnInk = RGBA(255, 255, 255, 1);   // text and icon on the selected nav item
clrOrange = RGBA(224, 102, 43, 1);
clrWhite = RGBA(255, 255, 255, 1);
clrClear = RGBA(0, 0, 0, 0);

// ---------- Pick lists ----------
nfTerminals = ["VI", "VA", "KE", "ED", "CA", "RG", "SK", "WI", "TO", "OT", "ML", "FR", "SJ", "MT", "HX", "SS"];
nfModes = ["FLEX", "STANDARD", "PREMIUM", "ROAD"];
nfEquipment = ["IM20", "IM40", "IM40 INSULATED", "IM53", "IM53 HEATED", "DRY 53 TANDEM", "DRY 53 TRIDEM", "PVT53 DRY", "PVT 53 HEATED", "53FT REEFER", "FLATBED 53FT TRIAXLE", "FLATBED SUPER B"];
nfDays = ["ALL", "MON", "TUE", "WED", "THU", "FRI", "SAT", "SUN"];

// Fuel table read once and reused by every cost label (refreshes after Refresh(FuelLibrary)).
nfFuel = ShowColumns(FuelLibrary, Title, FSC);

// ---------- Equipment ----------
EquipFt(Equip:Text):Number = If(StartsWith(Equip, "IM20"), 20, StartsWith(Equip, "IM40"), 40, 53);
CubicFt(Equip:Text):Number = Switch(EquipFt(Equip), 20, 1280, 40, 2350, 3830);
SkidSpots(Equip:Text):Number = Switch(EquipFt(Equip), 20, 10, 40, 20, 26);

// ---------- Cost maths (one definition used by every screen) ----------
// A program that isn't ALL-IN and has no FSC in the library.
FuelMissing(Fuel:Text):Boolean = !IsBlank(Fuel) && Fuel <> "ALL-IN" && IsBlank(LookUp(nfFuel, Title = Fuel).FSC);

// FSC % applied to the base: library FSC + fuel add-on − fuel discount (percentage points).
FscPct(Fuel:Text, FAdj:Number, FDisc:Number, WithFuel:Boolean):Number =
    If(
        !WithFuel || IsBlank(Fuel) || Fuel = "ALL-IN" || FuelMissing(Fuel),
        0,
        LookUp(nfFuel, Title = Fuel).FSC + Coalesce(FAdj, 0) - Coalesce(FDisc, 0)
    );

// Fuel on base first, then add-on, then discount. AdjType/DiscType are "%" or "$".
LHCost(Base:Number, Fuel:Text, FAdj:Number, FDisc:Number, Adj:Number, AdjType:Text, Disc:Number, DiscType:Text, WithFuel:Boolean):Number =
    With(
        {x: Coalesce(Base, 0) * (1 + FscPct(Fuel, FAdj, FDisc, WithFuel) / 100)},
        With(
            {y: If(AdjType = "%", x * (1 + Coalesce(Adj, 0) / 100), x + Coalesce(Adj, 0))},
            If(DiscType = "%", y * (1 - Coalesce(Disc, 0) / 100), y - Coalesce(Disc, 0))
        )
    );

                                                        =If(
                                                            !IsBlank(fulLblErr.Text),
                                                            Notify(fulLblErr.Text, NotificationType.Error),
                                                            With(
                                                                {
                                                                    r: Patch(
                                                                        FuelLibrary,
                                                                        If(varFuelEdit, LookUp(FuelLibrary, ID = varFuelRec.ID), Defaults(FuelLibrary)),
                                                                        {
                                                                            Title: Upper(Trim(fulTxtProgram.Text)),
                                                                            FSC: Value(fulTxtFsc.Text),
                                                                            EffectiveDate: fulDteEff.SelectedDate,
                                                                            Timestamp: Now(),
                                                                            AddedByEmail: User().Email,
                                                                            AddedByUser: User().FullName
                                                                        }
                                                                    )
                                                                },
                                                                If(
                                                                    IsError(r) || IsBlank(r.ID),
                                                                    Notify("The fuel program wasn't saved. Try again.", NotificationType.Error),
                                                                    Notify(r.Title & If(varFuelEdit, " updated.", " added."), NotificationType.Success);
                                                                    Refresh(FuelLibrary);
                                                                    Set(varFuelEdit, false);
                                                                    Set(varFuelRec, Blank());

                                                            PressedColor: =clrInk

                                                      - detBtnEditSave:
                                                          Control: Classic/Button@2.2.0
                                                          Properties:
                                                            BorderColor: =clrBlue
                                                            BorderThickness: =1
                                                            Color: =clrWhite
                                                            DisabledBorderColor: =clrLine
                                                            DisabledColor: =clrInk3
                                                            DisabledFill: =clrSoft
                                                            DisplayMode: =If(IsBlank(detLblEditErr.Text) && !varSavingEdit, DisplayMode.Edit, DisplayMode.Disabled)
                                                            Fill: =clrBlue
                                                            FillPortions: =0
                                                            Font: =Font.'Segoe UI'
                                                            FontWeight: =FontWeight.Semibold
                                                            Height: =38
                                                            HoverBorderColor: =clrBlueHover
                                                            HoverColor: =clrWhite
                                                            HoverFill: =clrBlue
                                                            LayoutMinHeight: =0
                                                            LayoutMinWidth: =0
                                                            OnSelect: |-
                                                              =If(
                                                                  !IsBlank(detLblEditErr.Text),
                                                                  Notify(detLblEditErr.Text, NotificationType.Error),
                                                                  !IsBlank(detLblEditWarn.Text),
                                                                  Set(varConfirmEdit, true),
                                                                  Set(varSavingEdit, true);
                                                                  With(
                                                                      {
                                                                          r: Patch(
                                                                              LinehaulRates,
                                                                              LookUp(LinehaulRates, ID = varRate.ID),
                                                                              {
                                                                                  Active: If(detTglEditActive.Value, "Yes", "No"),
                                                                                  EffectiveDate: detDteEditEff.SelectedDate,
                                                                                  ExpiryDate: detDteEditExp.SelectedDate,
                                                                                  Comments: detTxtEditNotes.Text
                                                                              }
                                                                          )
                                                                      },
                                                                      If(
                                                                          IsError(r) || IsBlank(r.ID),
                                                                          Notify("Changes weren't saved. Check your connection and try again.", NotificationType.Error),
                                                                          If(
                                                                              Upper(r.Active) = "YES",
                                                                              ForAll(
                                                                                  Filter(
                                                                                      AddColumns(
                                                                                          Filter(
                                                                                              LinehaulRates,
                                                                                              Title = r.Title && Destination = r.Destination && Carrier = r.Carrier &&
                                                                                              Mode = r.Mode && Equipment = r.Equipment
                                                                                          ),
                                                                                          IsOn, Upper(Trim(Active)) in ["YES", "TRUE"]
                                                                                      ),
                                                                                      IsOn && ID <> r.ID
                                                                                  ) As old,
                                                                                  Patch(LinehaulRates, LookUp(LinehaulRates, ID = old.ID), {Active: "No"})
                                                                              )
                                                                          );
                                                                          Set(
                                                                              varRate,
                                                                              Patch(
                                                                                  varRate,
                                                                                  {
                                                                                      Active: r.Active,
                                                                                      IsOn: Upper(r.Active) = "YES",
                                                                                      Expired: r.ExpiryDate < Today(),
                                                                                      EffectiveDate: r.EffectiveDate,
                                                                                      ExpiryDate: r.ExpiryDate,
                                                                                      Comments: r.Comments
                                                                                  }
                                                                              )
                                                                          );
                                                                          ClearCollect(
                                                                              colLaneRates,
                                                                              Sort(
                                                                                  AddColumns(Filter(LinehaulRates, Title = varRate.Title && Destination = varRate.Destination && Carrier = varRate.Carrier && Mode = varRate.Mode && Equipment = varRate.Equipment), IsOn, Upper(Trim(Active)) in ["YES", "TRUE"], LH, 0, NoFSC, false,
                                                                                             Expired, ExpiryDate < Today()),
                                                                                  Timestamp,
                                                                                  SortOrder.Descending
                                                                              )
                                                                          );
                                                                          Set(varEditRate, false);
                                                                          Notify("Changes saved.", NotificationType.Success)
                                                                      )
                                                                  );
                                                                  Set(varConfirmEdit, false);
                                                                  Set(varSavingEdit, false)
                                                              )
                                                            PaddingBottom: =0
                                                            PaddingLeft: =12
                                                            PaddingRight: =12
                                                            PaddingTop: =0
                                                            PressedBorderColor: =clrInk
                                                            PressedColor: =clrWhite
                                                            PressedFill: =clrInk
                                                            RadiusBottomLeft: =5
                                                            RadiusBottomRight: =5
                                                            RadiusTopLeft: =5
                                                            RadiusTopRight: =5
                                                            Size: =11
                                                            Text: =If(varSavingEdit, "Saving…", "Save changes")
                                                            Width: =140

                        PaddingTop: =0

                        Text: =detLblEditWarn.Text
                        VerticalAlign: =VerticalAlign.Top
                        Width: =Parent.Width - 40
                        Wrap: =true
                        X: =20
                        Y: =54
                  - detModalCancel:
                      Control: Classic/Button@2.2.0
                      Properties:
                        BorderColor: =clrLine
                        BorderThickness: =1
                        Color: =clrInk
                        DisabledBorderColor: =clrLine
                        DisabledColor: =clrInk3
                        DisabledFill: =clrSoft
                        Fill: =clrPanel
                        Font: =Font.'Segoe UI'
                        FontWeight: =FontWeight.Semibold
                        Height: =38
                        HoverBorderColor: =clrInk3
                        HoverColor: =clrInk
                        HoverFill: =clrPanel
                        OnSelect: =Set(varConfirmEdit, false)
                        PaddingBottom: =0
                        PaddingLeft: =12
                        PaddingRight: =12
                        PaddingTop: =0
                        PressedBorderColor: =clrInk3
                        PressedColor: =clrInk
                        PressedFill: =clrLine
                        RadiusBottomLeft: =5
                        RadiusBottomRight: =5
                        RadiusTopLeft: =5
                        RadiusTopRight: =5
                        Size: =11
                        Text: ="Go back"
                        Width: =120
                        X: =Parent.Width - 332
                        Y: =Parent.Height - 58
                  - detModalOk:
                      Control: Classic/Button@2.2.0
                      Properties:
                        BorderColor: =clrBlue
                        BorderThickness: =1
                        Color: =clrWhite
                        DisabledBorderColor: =clrLine
                        DisabledColor: =clrInk3
                        DisabledFill: =clrSoft
                        Fill: =clrBlue
                        Font: =Font.'Segoe UI'
                        FontWeight: =FontWeight.Semibold
                        Height: =38
                        HoverBorderColor: =clrBlueHover
                        HoverColor: =clrWhite
                        HoverFill: =clrBlue
                        OnSelect: |-
                          =Set(varSavingEdit, true);
                          With(
                              {
                                  r: Patch(
                                      LinehaulRates,
                                      LookUp(LinehaulRates, ID = varRate.ID),
                                      {
                                          Active: If(detTglEditActive.Value, "Yes", "No"),
                                          EffectiveDate: detDteEditEff.SelectedDate,
                                          ExpiryDate: detDteEditExp.SelectedDate,
                                          Comments: detTxtEditNotes.Text
                                      }
                                  )
                              },
                              If(
                                  IsError(r) || IsBlank(r.ID),
                                  Notify("Changes weren't saved. Check your connection and try again.", NotificationType.Error),
                                  If(
                                      Upper(r.Active) = "YES",
                                      ForAll(
                                          Filter(
                                              AddColumns(
                                                  Filter(
                                                      LinehaulRates,
                                                      Title = r.Title && Destination = r.Destination && Carrier = r.Carrier &&
                                                      Mode = r.Mode && Equipment = r.Equipment
                                                  ),
                                                  IsOn, Upper(Trim(Active)) in ["YES", "TRUE"]
                                              ),
                                              IsOn && ID <> r.ID
                                          ) As old,
                                          Patch(LinehaulRates, LookUp(LinehaulRates, ID = old.ID), {Active: "No"})
                                      )
                                  );
                                  Set(
                                      varRate,
                                      Patch(
                                          varRate,
                                          {
                                              Active: r.Active,
                                              IsOn: Upper(r.Active) = "YES",
                                              Expired: r.ExpiryDate < Today(),
                                              EffectiveDate: r.EffectiveDate,
                                              ExpiryDate: r.ExpiryDate,
                                              Comments: r.Comments
                                          }
                                      )
                                  );
                                  ClearCollect(
                                      colLaneRates,
                                      Sort(
                                          AddColumns(Filter(LinehaulRates, Title = varRate.Title && Destination = varRate.Destination && Carrier = varRate.Carrier && Mode = varRate.Mode && Equipment = varRate.Equipment), IsOn, Upper(Trim(Active)) in ["YES", "TRUE"], LH, 0, NoFSC, false,
                                                     Expired, ExpiryDate < Today()),
                                          Timestamp,
                                          SortOrder.Descending
                                      )
                                  );
                                  Set(varEditRate, false);
                                  Notify("Changes saved.", NotificationType.Success)
                              )
                          );
                          Set(varConfirmEdit, false);
                          Set(varSavingEdit, false)
                        PaddingBottom: =0
                        PaddingLeft: =12
                        PaddingRight: =12
                        PaddingTop: =0
                        PressedBorderColor: =clrInk
                        PressedColor: =clrWhite
                        PressedFill: =clrInk
                        RadiusBottomLeft: =5
                        RadiusBottomRight: =5
                        RadiusTopLeft: =5
                        RadiusTopRight: =5
                        Size: =11
                        Text: ="Save changes"
                        Width: =180
                        X: =Parent.Width - 200
                        Y: =Parent.Height - 58

                                    DisabledFill: =clrSoft

                                    Fill: =clrBlue
                                    Font: =Font.'Segoe UI'
                                    Height: =44
                                    HoverBorderColor: =clrBlueHover
                                    HoverColor: =clrWhite
                                    HoverFill: =clrBlue
                                    LayoutMinHeight: =0
                                    LayoutMinWidth: =0
                                    OnSelect: |-
                                      =If(
                                          !IsBlank(entLblErrors.Text),
                                          Notify(entLblErrors.Text, NotificationType.Error),
                                          !IsBlank(entLblConfirm.Text),
                                          Set(varConfirmSave, true),
                                          Set(varSaving, true);
                                          If(
                                              !IsBlank(entLblErrors.Text),
                                              Notify(entLblErrors.Text, NotificationType.Error),
                                              With(
                                                  {
                                                      n: Patch(
                                                          LinehaulRates,
                                                          Defaults(LinehaulRates),
                                                          {
                                                              Title: varOrigin,
                                                              Destination: varDest,
                                                              Mode: varMode,
                                                              Carrier: varCarrier,
                                                              Equipment: varEquip,
                                                              Fuel: varFuel,
                                                              ServiceDays: If(
                                                                  "ALL" in colDays.Value,
                                                                  "ALL",
                                                                  Concat(Filter(nfDays, Value in colDays.Value), Value, ";")
                                                              ),
                                                              EffectiveDate: entDteEff.SelectedDate,
                                                              ExpiryDate: entDteExpiry.SelectedDate,
                                                              Base: Value(entTxtBase.Text),
                                                              Adj: Value(entTxtAdj.Text),
                                                              AdjType: If(IsBlank(Trim(entTxtAdj.Text)), "", If(entTglAdjPct.Value, "%", "$")),
                                                              Discount: Value(entTxtDisc.Text),
                                                              DiscountType: If(IsBlank(Trim(entTxtDisc.Text)), "", If(entTglDiscPct.Value, "%", "$")),
                                                              F_Adj: Value(entTxtFAdj.Text),
                                                              F_AdjType: If(IsBlank(Trim(entTxtFAdj.Text)), "", "%"),
                                                              F_Discount: Value(entTxtFDisc.Text),
                                                              F_DiscountType: If(IsBlank(Trim(entTxtFDisc.Text)), "", "%"),
                                                              Comments: entTxtNotes.Text,
                                                              Active: If(entTglActive.Value, "Yes", "No"),
                                                              SubmittedBy: User().FullName,
                                                              SubmittedEmail: User().Email,
                                                              Timestamp: Now()
                                                          }
                                                      )
                                                  },
                                                  If(
                                                      IsError(n) || IsBlank(n.ID),
                                                      Notify("The rate wasn't saved. Check your connection and try again.", NotificationType.Error),
                                                      If(
                                                          Upper(n.Active) = "YES",
                                                          ForAll(
                                                              Filter(
                                                                  AddColumns(
                                                                      Filter(
                                                                          LinehaulRates,
                                                                          Title = n.Title && Destination = n.Destination && Carrier = n.Carrier &&
                                                                          Mode = n.Mode && Equipment = n.Equipment
                                                                      ),
                                                                      IsOn, Upper(Trim(Active)) in ["YES", "TRUE"]
                                                                  ),
                                                                  IsOn && ID <> n.ID
                                                              ) As old,
                                                              Patch(LinehaulRates, LookUp(LinehaulRates, ID = old.ID), {Active: "No"})
                                                          )
                                                      );
                                                      Notify("Rate saved for " & n.Title & n.Destination & " " & n.Carrier & ".", NotificationType.Success);
                                                      Select(entBtnReset)
                                                  )
                                              )
                                          );
                                          Set(varSaving, false)
                                      )

                                    PaddingRight: =12
                                    PaddingTop: =0

            Text: |-
              =With(
                  {
                      miss: Concat(
                          Filter(
                              Table(
                                  {f: "Origin", ok: !IsBlank(varOrigin)},
                                  {f: "Destination", ok: !IsBlank(varDest)},
                                  {f: "Mode", ok: !IsBlank(varMode)},
                                  {f: "Equipment", ok: !IsBlank(varEquip)},
                                  {f: "Carrier", ok: !IsBlank(varCarrier)},
                                  {f: "Service days", ok: CountRows(colDays) > 0},
                                  {f: "Base rate", ok: !IsBlank(Trim(entTxtBase.Text))},
                                  {f: "Fuel program", ok: !IsBlank(varFuel)}
                              ),
                              !ok
                          ),
                          f,
                          ", "
                      )
                  },
                  Trim(
                      If(!IsBlank(miss), "Still needed: " & miss & ". ", "") &
                      If(entNumOk.Text <> "ok", "Cost fields take numbers only (no $, % or minus). ", "") &
                      If(
                          IsBlank(entDteEff.SelectedDate) || IsBlank(entDteExpiry.SelectedDate) ||
                          entDteExpiry.SelectedDate <= entDteEff.SelectedDate,
                          "Expiry must be after the effective date. ", ""
                      ) &
                      If(
                          !IsBlank(varOrigin) && varOrigin = varDest,
                          "Origin and destination can't be the same. ", ""
                      ) &
                      If(
                          entNumOk.Text = "ok" && !IsBlank(Trim(entTxtBase.Text)) && Value(entTxtBase.Text) <= 0,
                          "Base rate must be more than $0. ", ""
                      ) &
                      If(
                          entNumOk.Text = "ok" && entTglDiscPct.Value && Coalesce(Value(entTxtDisc.Text), 0) >= 100,
                          "A % discount must be under 100. ", ""
                      ) &
                      If(
                          FuelMissing(varFuel),
                          "The fuel program has no FSC. Fix it in the Fuel library first. ", ""
                      ) &
                      If(
                          entNumOk.Text = "ok" && !IsBlank(varFuel) &&
                          FscPct(varFuel, Value(entTxtFAdj.Text), Value(entTxtFDisc.Text), true) < 0,
                          "Fuel discount is bigger than the FSC. ", ""
                      ) &
                      If(
                          !IsBlank(entCalc.Text) && Value(entCalc.Text) <= 0,
                          "The discount wipes out the whole cost. Check the discount. ", ""
                      )
                  )
              )

                            Properties:
                              Color: =clrInk
                              Font: =Font.'Segoe UI'
                              FontWeight: =If(ThisItem.Value = Switch(varPick, "Origin", varOrigin, "Destination", varDest, "Mode", varMode, "Equipment", varEquip, "Carrier", varCarrier, "Fuel program", varFuel, ""), FontWeight.Bold, FontWeight.Normal)
                              Height: =48
                              OnSelect: =Select(Parent)
                              PaddingBottom: =0
                              PaddingLeft: =20
                              PaddingRight: =0
                              PaddingTop: =0
                              Size: =11
                              Text: =ThisItem.Value
                              Width: =Parent.TemplateWidth - 60
                        - entPickRowCheck:
                            Control: Label@2.5.1
                            Properties:
                              Align: =Align.Center
                              Color: =clrBlue
                              Font: =Font.'Segoe UI'
                              FontWeight: =FontWeight.Bold
                              Height: =48
                              OnSelect: =Select(Parent)
                              PaddingBottom: =0
                              PaddingLeft: =0
                              PaddingRight: =0
                              PaddingTop: =0
                              Text: ="✓"
                              Visible: =ThisItem.Value = Switch(varPick, "Origin", varOrigin, "Destination", varDest, "Mode", varMode, "Equipment", varEquip, "Carrier", varCarrier, "Fuel program", varFuel, "")
                              Width: =40
                              X: =Parent.TemplateWidth - 56
                        - entPickRowSep:
                            Control: Rectangle@2.3.0
                            Properties:
                              Fill: =clrSoft
                              Height: =1
                              Width: =Parent.TemplateWidth
                              Y: =47
                  - entPickFootLine:
                      Control: Rectangle@2.3.0
                      Properties:
                        Fill: =clrLine
                        Height: =1
                        Visible: =varPick = "Carrier"
                        Width: =Parent.Width
                        Y: =Parent.Height - 80
                  - entPickNew:
                      Control: Classic/TextInput@2.3.2
                      Properties:
                        BorderColor: =clrLine
                        BorderThickness: =1
                        Color: =clrInk
                        Default: =""
                        Fill: =clrPanel
                        Font: =Font.'Segoe UI'
                        HintText: ="Not listed? Type a new carrier"
                        HoverBorderColor: =clrInk3
                        HoverFill: =clrPanel
                        PaddingBottom: =6
                        PaddingRight: =12
                        PaddingTop: =6
                        PressedBorderColor: =clrBlue
                        PressedFill: =clrPanel
                        Size: =11
                        Visible: =varPick = "Carrier"
                        Width: =Parent.Width - 180
                        X: =20
                        Y: =Parent.Height - 60
                  - entPickAdd:
                      Control: Classic/Button@2.2.0
                      Properties:
                        BorderColor: =clrBlue
                        BorderThickness: =1
                        Color: =clrWhite
                        DisabledBorderColor: =clrLine
                        DisabledColor: =clrInk3
                        DisabledFill: =clrSoft
                        Fill: =clrBlue
                        Font: =Font.'Segoe UI'
                        HoverBorderColor: =clrBlueHover
                        HoverColor: =clrWhite
                        HoverFill: =clrBlue
                        OnSelect: |-
                          =With(
                              {c: Upper(Trim(entPickNew.Text))},
                              If(
                                  IsBlank(c),
                                  Notify("Type a carrier name first.", NotificationType.Warning),
                                  !IsBlank(LookUp(Carriers, Title = c)),
                                  Set(varCarrier, c);
                                  Notify(c & " was already in the list, so it's selected.", NotificationType.Information);
                                  Set(varPick, ""),
                                  With(
                                      {r: Patch(Carriers, Defaults(Carriers), {Title: c})},
                                      If(
                                          IsError(r),
                                          Notify("The carrier wasn't added. Try again.", NotificationType.Error),
                                          Set(varCarrier, c);
                                          Notify(c & " added and selected.", NotificationType.Success);
                                          Set(varPick, "")
                                      )
                                  )
                              )
                          )
                        PaddingBottom: =0
                        PaddingLeft: =12
                        PaddingRight: =12
                        PaddingTop: =0
                        PressedBorderColor: =clrInk
                        PressedColor: =clrWhite
                        PressedFill: =clrInk
                        RadiusBottomLeft: =5
                        RadiusBottomRight: =5
                        RadiusTopLeft: =5
                        RadiusTopRight: =5
                        Size: =11
                        Text: ="Add carrier"
                        Visible: =varPick = "Carrier"
                        Width: =128
                        X: =Parent.Width - 148
                        Y: =Parent.Height - 60
